title: Current contracts · v1.22
---

# XYZ.js v1.22.1 — current contracts

**Normative for package 1.22.1, Apache-2.0, browser runtime, zero runtime dependencies.** npm remains unpublished. This page describes current supported profiles including P104–P118, not an acceptance report or upstream compatibility promise. [GitHub v1.16](https://github.com/JS-PACKAGE/XYZ.js/releases/tag/v1.16) is published after the existing CI gate, with its downloaded archive/checksum verified; the historical v1.14 archive does not contain P104–P118. Historical dates, test counts, release versions and originally excluded features remain in [ACCEPTANCE](https://github.com/JS-PACKAGE/XYZ.js/blob/main/ACCEPTANCE.md). English / 繁體中文 / 日本語：目前契約／現在の契約。Older exclusions do not override the current profiles below.

## Public API and distribution

The supported public entry is `xyz.js` (or the complete built tree's `engine/src/index.js` on the static site). It exports core, graphics, math, assets, input and audio; ECS is an internal model, not a separate root export. Use the generated **API v1.22.1** portal for exact classes, types, methods and overloads. Its search includes API names, comments and this document; inherited members can be shown with the visibility filters.

`pnpm docs:api` generates `.vite/site/api/1.22.1/` and its documentation landing pages. `pnpm build:site` builds the examples and the same searchable API into the complete `.vite/site/` distribution. Serve over HTTP/HTTPS and open `docs/` or `api/1.22.1/`; generated HTML is not tracked or included in the engine tarball. Relative API links and search assets stay within the version directory, so deployment under a path prefix does not require URL rewriting. Source documentation is not a claim that the hosted site has been deployed.

For standalone consumers, build and pack, then use `node scripts/create-game.mjs /absolute/my-game --template 2d --package /absolute/xyz.js-1.22.1.tgz --name my-game` (or `3d`). Deploy the complete starter `dist/`. No-bundler engine deployment likewise requires the complete engine `dist/`, including the unchanged official `dist/vendor/opm/` distribution and licenses.

CommonJS and ESM use separate constructor graphs. Choose one format for every
engine object in an application; cross-format Texture/Scene/Mesh instances are
not interchangeable. Canonical type declarations do not imply shared runtime
identity. CDN metadata selects ESM; the GitHub release does not publish npm.

## Capability matrix

These are implementation profiles, **not three-backend equivalence or physical qualification**. Check `game.graphics.capabilities` and optional renderer methods instead of inferring support from the backend name.

| Surface                                                                                    | WebGPU                                          | WebGL2                                          | Canvas2D / shared boundary                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Sprite, atlas, text, HUD/UI, raster paths, isolation, masks, basic blends, render textures | Supported                                       | Supported                                       | Supported; raster paths/text are texture-backed, not native vector tessellation                       |
| Native Material2D, Filter2D, Mesh2D, shader postprocessing                                 | Supported, native WGSL                          | Supported, native GLSL                          | Explicitly unsupported; no software shader/3D fallback                                                |
| Mesh/PBR, instancing, skinning, shadows, 3D postprocessing                                 | Supported                                       | Supported subject to capabilities/extensions    | No visible 3D; HDR/weighted transparency on GL require float color attachments                        |
| Native compressed textures / supplied mips                                                 | Capability-gated formats                        | Capability-gated formats                        | Native sources rejected; select a real raster fallback                                                |
| GPU 3D particles                                                                           | Native analytic vertex particles; prepare first | Native analytic vertex particles; prepare first | Explicitly unsupported; CPU Sprite-particle profiles remain available                                 |
| Initialization                                                                             | `auto` tries WebGPU → WebGL2 → Canvas2D         | Forced backend never switches                   | `auto` fallback is initialization-only                                                                |
| Runtime loss                                                                               | Default same-backend recovery                   | Default same-backend recovery                   | Recreate renderer-owned targets/snapshots after recovery; failure or `recoverGraphics:false` is fatal |

WebGPU and AudioWorklet require a secure origin (localhost is allowed). Device availability is not implied by `navigator.gpu`; the current public compute profile is the bounded WebGPU-only ComputeProgram/ComputeBuffer API. Native shader/particle descriptors must be prepared before rendering; callers own their destruction after removing consumers. Auto presentation uses a copy to the original canvas and is not a performance-equivalent forced backend.

Mesh programs/pipelines are lazy, feature-keyed and bounded to 64 cached entries.
Zero-strength physical lobes are omitted from ordinary shader source. Cold synchronous
draws still compile synchronously; explicit preparation uses available parallel/async
driver compilation. Native hooks retain conservative full shading and recovery preparation.

## Shared subsystem profiles

| Surface                         | Current support and important bounds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Lifecycle / simulation          | Game/Scene ownership, atomic prepared-scene publication, cooperative abort, fixed gameplay updates and opt-in physics interpolation. Time is seconds; pause/hidden time does not accumulate. Do not update physics again inside `fixedUpdate`.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Assets / glTF                   | Task-based preload, manifest/bundles, ResourcePool leases/scopes, bounded streamed reads and cleanup. glTF triangles, UV0/UV1, independent per-map texture transforms, four/eight skin influences, morph and `COLOR_0`; `COLOR_1` and unsupported required extensions reject. Meshopt is built in; Draco/Basis use explicitly supplied external codecs.                                                                        |
| Asset production                | Semantic platform bundles contain capability-selected variants and a codec-free raster fallback. Preflight accepts `TEXCOORD_0` and `TEXCOORD_1`, requires each textured primitive's selected stream, and keeps material-map transforms independent. Default KTX2 decoding is base-level RGBA8; native sources preserve supported payloads/mips.                                                                               |
| Physics2D                       | Dynamic/static/kinematic bodies, sleep, five joints, static concave decomposition/chains, convex character sweep/slide/platform carry and bounded relative translation/rotation CCD. Sensors are discrete; no dynamic concave/compound/deforming sweeps.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Physics3D / navigation          | Bounded primitive dynamic bodies, joints, relative/angular CCD, capsule character/crouch/moving support, queries; graphs, polygon navmeshes, layered/partitioned sampled worlds and collision-derived navigation use documented work/storage quotas, not unlimited world size.                                                                                                                                                 |
| Animation                       | Ordered layers, masks, reference-relative additive animation, fades/crossfades, state machines, 1D/triangulated 2D blend trees, two-bone IK, GPU skinning with lazy CPU queries and conservative animated bounds.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Content / input / accessibility | Tiled JSON including bounded infinite chunks/groups/parallax/animation/templates; input contexts, remapping and portable settings/saves; semantic-only DOM focus/activation mirror. A semantic mirror is not DOM-rendered gameplay or assistive-technology certification.                                                                                                                                                      |
| Audio                           | Immutable official OPM.js v1.13.1 bytes (tag v1.13.1), eight slots including release on one manager-owned AudioContext shared with native samples/streams (P137). Gesture unlock precedes decode/play; an OPM start failure leaves samples/streams usable (`opmState`/`opmError`). Game pause does not automatically pause audio. Native effects/automation/ducking/world bindings do not imply audible hardware verification. |
| Scale / rendering budgets       | Visibility/LOD/HLOD, streaming, workers, bounded light/shadow selection and observability. Worker results still incur main-thread Geometry validation/copy. Per Scene: up to 1024 point and 1024 spot lights; per draw: 32 each; shadows: four cascades/eight point/eight spot.                                                                                                                                                |
| Deployment / compatibility      | 1.x additive profiles, optional renderer extensions, standalone 2D/3D starters, strict-CSP hosting and opt-in offline assets. Offline support does not cache arbitrary missing resources or relax origin/security rules.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Official OPM.js v1.13.1 (tag `v1.13.1`) normalizes voices to v8 internally; the engine-exposed `OPMVoice` retains the published `version: 1` contract. `game.audio.opm` is the engine's legacy compatibility facade backed by the first official instance, not that native instance itself; it is available only while `opmState` is `'ready'`. It preserves writable context/node handles and a mutable voice `Map` with v1 voices. Direct escape-hatch use still bypasses engine budgeting and lifecycle management; managed playback and teardown continue to use official `panic()`/`dispose()` internally without modifying vendor bytes.

Since P137 `AudioManager` owns one AudioContext; the eight OPM instances borrow it through the official `context` option and never suspend or close it. `unlock()` resolves once that context runs even if OPM cannot start (insecure origin, missing vendor files, blocked worklet): the failure is reported through the Game `error` event and `opmError`, OPM asset playback throws, and samples/streams still play. `unlock()` rejects only when no AudioContext can be created or resumed. Executed evidence is in the P137 ACCEPTANCE section.

P138 adds the opt-in `opmModuleUrl` Game option to load official OPM.js from a commit-pinned CDN or another same-origin path instead of the vendored copy. It accepts HTTPS (or same-origin HTTP) module URLs only and requires the module to report the vendored version; it cannot integrity-check CDN bytes, and the page's CSP must allow that origin. The vendored copy remains the default and is still packaged. Evidence is in the P138 ACCEPTANCE section.

'mungbeans' | 'thomsonite' | 'dalmatianstone' | 'nickelsilver' | 'hopsack' | 'monkscloth' | 'lyocell' | 'sateen' | 'surah' | 'serge' | 'batiste' | 'ramie'`) and `ProceduralMaterialOptions` (`size?: number; seed?: number; contrast?: number; roughnessBias?: number; repeats?: number; tint?: readonly [number, number, number]`). `tint` multiplies the authored sRGB pigment per channel (each finite 0–4); mortar/grout tones and the normal, metallic-roughness and occlusion maps are unchanged, and omitting it keeps output byte-identical. Passing `workerPool` (from `createProceduralWorkerPool()`, a borrowed `NativeWorkerPool` running the published `workers/procedural-material.worker.js` or an app module installing `trustedProceduralMapsJob` as `procedural.maps`) moves generation off the calling thread with byte-identical output; without it generation stays synchronous. The caller owns and destroys the pool; texture acquisition still happens on the main thread. A caller-owned `ProceduralMapCache({ maxBytes?, maxEntries? })` (default 64 MiB / 32 entries, LRU) passed as `cache` reuses generated CPU maps for identical kind/size/seed/contrast/roughnessBias/tint, shares concurrent identical generations and never caches failures; each preset still creates and owns its own textures, and `clear()` drops retained maps. Set `heightMap: true` to also generate a linear height map (R, 0 deepest–1 highest); `createMaterial()` binds it as `opticalMaps.heightTexture`, which PBR parallax (`finish.heightScale`) then samples instead of normal Z. It does not enable parallax by itself, is included in cache keys and worker transfers, and is destroyed with the preset. `await ProceduralMaterial.create(kind, options?)` generates deterministic, seamlessly periodic immutable maps once on the CPU, without external assets or new dependencies. `size` is an integer 32–1024 (default 256); `seed` is an unsigned 32-bit integer 0–4294967295 (default 1). Invalid inputs reject.

The kind union additionally accepts `chainstitch`: five columns of six linked, tapered hollow gold embroidery loops above dark blue woven backing. Its 0.12 m tile retains periodic opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and existing ownership; no yarn geometry, transparent holes or cloth simulation.

The kind union additionally accepts `wingnuts`: sixteen alternating quarter-turned silver wing nuts, with paired raised oblique wings, round hubs and recessed central bores above dark steel backing. The 0.12 m tile uses opaque metal maps and metallic factor 1, optional height and existing ownership; no nut geometry, transparent holes, real threads or mechanical simulation.

`pomegranateslice` adds nine red-rimmed round cross sections, each with six recessed chambers containing seven separate raised red arils, pale central pith and dividing membranes. The 0.15 m tile is opaque dielectric (metallic 0), with existing maps, optional height and ownership; no fruit geometry, transparency, transmission or biological simulation.

`pawprinttiles` adds sixteen ivory glazed square tiles with recessed cobalt paw stamps: four separate oval toe pads and a three-lobed lower palm, above rough neutral grout. One UV tile represents 0.6 m. Opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership stay unchanged; no tile geometry, physical stamping or animal simulation.

`dovetailjoint` adds two rows of four outward-flared trapezoidal wood tongues with recessed straight shoulders, oblique sides and flat tips. Adjacent boards alternate longitudinal and transverse grain with continuous board ownership across rows. The 0.48 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no joinery geometry or structural simulation.

`starsocketbolts` adds sixteen domed silver bolt caps with closed-bottom six-lobed star recesses, alternating by 30°. Socket floors remain above the dark steel backing. The 0.12 m tile uses opaque metal maps and default metallic 1, optional height and existing ownership; no bolt geometry, transparent holes, real threads or mechanical simulation.

`sanddollar` adds nine pale cream flat oval shells with five closed petal-outline grooves and five narrow radial lunules between petals. One tile covers 0.3 m. Opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership remain unchanged; lunules reveal shaded opaque backing, not transparent holes. Fine petal grooves are easier to inspect at larger texture/preview resolutions.

`brogueleather` adds nine six-petaled oval-punch medallions with round centers and double rows of small border holes on warm brown leather. Recesses reveal rough opaque dark backing. The 0.18 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no leather geometry, transparent openings or material simulation.

`clovebuds` adds sixteen brown dried flower buds, with raised round heads, four narrow calyx lobes and slender stems alternating by 90°. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; gaps are shaded opaque backing, not spice geometry or biological/food simulation.

`starcrosstiles` alternates sixteen teal eight-pointed stars with ivory cross-shaped glazed tiles and recessed neutral grout. Both straight and diagonal edges have smooth shoulders, including concave joins and adjacent star tips. The 0.8 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no tile geometry, transparent gaps or paving simulation.

`maplesamara` adds sixteen pairs of golden-brown V-shaped wings, flared toward their ends, with darker raised kernels at the roots and a short connecting stalk. Adjacent pairs flip by 180°; fine veins remain on the wing membranes. The 0.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no fruit geometry, translucent membranes, flight or biological simulation.

`lenoweave` adds six paired cream/ochre warp groups twisting between six horizontal wefts. Warp-over-warp and warp-over-weft ordering alternate; open cells show low opaque dark backing. The 0.06 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no yarn geometry, transparent mesh or cloth simulation.

`circlips` adds sixteen silver open C-shaped retaining rings, each with two terminal round lugs and recessed plier bores, alternating by 180°. Centers, gaps and bores reveal dark opaque steel backing. The 0.08 m tile retains metal maps/default metallic 1, optional height and ownership; no clip geometry, transparent holes, spring behavior or mechanical simulation.

`tuliptiles` adds sixteen ivory glazed square tiles with cobalt three-pointed tulip crowns, slender stems and two pointed leaves. Motifs sit slightly above the glaze; rough neutral grout is recessed. The 0.6 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no flower/tile geometry, transparent joints or plant simulation.

`gingerslice` adds sixteen knobbly rhizome cross sections composed of four joined unequal lobes, alternating by 90°. Pale yellow fibrous flesh stands above the thin, darker, rougher brown rind; concave necks distinguish them from round slices or perforated lotus root. The 0.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no rhizome geometry, transparency or food/plant simulation.

`fivebarplate` adds sixteen groups of five parallel round-ended silver ribs, alternating between ±45° over a rougher metal sheet, distinct from single-lozenge `diamondplate`. The 0.6 m tile retains metal maps/default metallic 1, optional height and ownership; no plate geometry, actual slip resistance or structural physics. Larger texture/preview resolutions help resolve individual thin ribs.

`cinnamonswirl` adds sixteen golden bun cross sections with a continuous recessed brown cinnamon spiral, a filled center and a darker baked rim, alternating by 180°. Its 0.32 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no food geometry, transparency or baking simulation.

`wheatears` adds sixteen stylized golden wheat ears, each with five pairs of grooved kernels, ten slender awns and a central rachis, alternating by 180°. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no plant geometry, transparency, growth or wind simulation. Larger texture/preview resolutions help resolve the thin awns and grooves.

`scalemail` adds six columns by eight staggered rows of steel scales, with upper rows covering lower rows, raised central ribs, round rivets and rolled rims. Dark gaps retain opaque steel backing, unlike organic `fishscales` or ring-shaped `chainmail`. The 0.24 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no scale geometry, transparent holes, collision or protective physics.

`trihexagonaltiles` adds 56 blue hexagonal tiles and 112 ivory triangles in alternating orientations, separated by shared recessed neutral grout; the hexagons sit slightly higher and smoother. The 0.84 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no tile geometry, collision or construction simulation.

`burlwood` adds stylized brown-gold figured wood with warped closed-contour grain, nine offset elliptical eyes and darker, slightly recessed, rougher piths. Seeded grain varies around fixed eye locations, unlike straight wood fibers or small bird's-eye maple figures. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no wood geometry, species identification, ring dating or growth simulation.

`slottedmetal` adds 8×4 staggered vertical round-ended wells in a brushed steel sheet, with machined beveled walls and dark opaque steel floors. Matching axis units preserves circular end caps despite rectangular pitch; this differs from round `perforatedmetal` holes and horizontal wood slots. The 0.4 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no through-hole geometry, transparency, ventilation or structural physics.

`peppercorns` adds 25 stylized dark-brown dried berries with domed bodies, shallow wrinkled skin and darker recessed, rougher stalk scars, distinct from clove stems/calyxes or coffee-bean slits. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no berry geometry, transparency or food/plant simulation. Its low-contrast skin is intentionally subtle, not a photogrammetric scan.

`castellatednuts` adds 4×4 alternating-orientation steel hex nuts with round central bores, six recessed radial crown slots and six raised segments. The inner ring remains continuous and the slots have opaque steel floors, unlike uncut `hexnuts` or external crown-cap flutes. The 0.08 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no nut geometry, transparent through-holes, screw threads or fastening physics.

`farfalle` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical golden bow-tie pasta pieces with connected narrow waists, flared wings, converging pleats and scalloped end cuts, distinct from filled ravioli pillows or hollow macaroni. The 0.16 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no pasta geometry, transparency, cooking or soft-body physics.

`cardamompods` adds 4×4 stylized olive-green closed pods alternating diagonally, with pointed tapered ends, domed skins, converging longitudinal ribs and channels, and attached short stalks. This differs from clove stems/calyxes and opened pistachios. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no pod geometry, transparency, opening animation or plant/food simulation.

`brickarches` adds a 4×4 decorative red-brick arch frieze: each semicircle has nine wedge-shaped voussoirs, radial mortar joints and two piers with three bed-jointed courses each. Seeded brick tones fade into common recessed mortar; openings remain dark opaque backing. The 2.4 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no building geometry, transparent doorways, load-bearing or collision physics.

`knottednet` adds a 6×6 tan cord net with bowed sagging spans, raised crossed ties, tightened knot creases and dark recessed mesh centers. Unlike straight metallic wiremesh, diagonal lace or staggered macrame, its strands bow continuously between a square knot lattice. The 0.3 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no net geometry, transparent openings, tension or cloth physics.

`fiddleheads` adds 4×4 alternating mirrored green fern-shoot motifs with tapered one-and-a-half-turn coils, open spiral gaps and attached bent stems, distinct from expanded fern pinnae. Precomputed smooth capsule unions preserve connected stems without angular ownership seams. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no plant geometry, transparency, growth or branch physics. This is a stylized motif, not botanical identification or a photogrammetric scan.

`cowrieshells` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical ivory cowrie undersides, with domed oval shells, dark narrow recessed apertures and nine raised smoother teeth on each opposing lip. Tooth rows do not bridge the aperture; this differs from scallop ribs and nautilus coils. The 0.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no shell geometry, transparent apertures or marine-life simulation.

`cotterpins` adds 4×4 alternating upright/inverted steel split pins, with elliptical loop eyes, two separated shafts and unequal outward-bent tails. Compact ring and capsule unions keep the wire connected while preserving dark opaque eye/shaft gaps. The 0.12 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no hardware geometry, transparent holes, fastening or bending physics.

`seedstitch` adds a sage-green 12×12 checker of open V-shaped knit stitches and horizontal purl bumps, alternating across both rows and columns. Smooth finite yarn capsules retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.12 m tile; no yarn geometry, transparency or cloth simulation.

`mangosteenslice` adds 4×4 thick purple-red rinds enclosing six rounded ivory pulp lobes, joined at the center and separated by recessed radial gaps; neighboring slices turn by 30°. Smooth compact ellipse unions retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.32 m tile; no fruit geometry, transparent openings or botanical simulation.

`pickettiles` adds a staggered field of teal glazed elongated hexagons: 36 pointed tiles per 0.9 m UV square, nominally 0.1×0.3 m before grout/bevel. Smooth products of six half-planes retain straight sides, tapered tips and recessed neutral grout, with seeded per-tile tones. Opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership remain unchanged; no tile geometry, transparency or construction simulation.

`squarewashers` adds 4×4 closed square steel frames alternating by 45°, with beveled straight outer sides, four retained corners and circular recessed center bores. The 0.16 m tile retains opaque metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; bores remain dark opaque steel backing. No washer geometry, transparent holes, fastening or load simulation.

`eucalyptusleaf` adds 4×4 blue-green lanceolate leaves tilted alternately by ±45°, with gently curved blades, tapered tips, raised midribs, five pairs of oblique secondary veins and connected short petioles. Compact polynomial blade/stem fields retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.48 m tile; no leaf geometry, transparency, wind or botanical simulation.

`twillweave` adds a 16×16 balanced two-over-two twill: light tan weft crossings and dark brown warp crossings appear in pairs, shifted one yarn per row to form continuous diagonals. Rounded yarn profiles and localized over/under exposure retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.16 m tile; no yarn geometry, transparent gaps or cloth simulation.

`pumpkinseeds` adds 4×4 pale seed hulls tilted alternately by ±45°, with tapered pointed ends, raised perimeter rims and intact lower central faces. Nested compact polynomial masks retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.12 m tile; no seed geometry, transparent hulls, cracking or botanical simulation.

`spurgears` adds 4×4 stylized steel gears alternating by 15°, with twelve beveled external block teeth, recessed circular bores and raised annular hubs. Smooth tooth/body unions retain opaque metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership on a 0.2 m tile; bore/gullet gaps remain dark opaque steel backing. No gear geometry, transparent holes, involute-profile certification or transmission simulation.

`diamondcheckertiles` adds 32 square glazed tiles rotated 45°, alternating black and cream along both diagonal axes, with softened bevels, recessed neutral grout and seeded face tones. Quotient-lattice ownership remains periodic across both UV edges; the 1.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership. No tile geometry, transparency or physics.

`appleslice` adds 4×4 apple cross sections alternating by 36°, with thin red-brown peel, pale flesh and five separate recessed seed chambers containing darker raised seeds below the uncut flesh. Its 0.4 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership; distinct from the intact exterior in `appleskin`. No fruit geometry, transparent chambers, cutting, juice or botanical simulation.

`paperclips` adds 4×4 silver steel wire clips alternating by ±45°, with three connected legs, two nested return bends and two open wire ends around recessed slots. Compact capsule/ring unions retain opaque metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership on a 0.24 m tile; slots remain dark opaque steel backing. No wire geometry, transparency or clamping simulation.

`ivyleaf` adds 4×4 green three-lobed ivy leaves alternating by ±45°, with pointed lobes, two deep notches, three raised palmate veins and connected short petioles. Compact polynomial lobe unions and masked vein projections retain opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.32 m tile. No leaf geometry, transparency, wind animation or botanical simulation.

`ribknit` adds 12×12 rose-brown stitches in alternating vertical columns of raised V-shaped knit stitches and lower horizontal purl bumps. Column ownership stays fixed across rows, unlike `seedstitch`; compact yarn supports preserve periodic opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.12 m tile. No yarn geometry, transparent holes, elasticity or cloth simulation.

`pillowtiles` adds 6×6 ivory glazed rounded-square tiles with continuous convex cushion faces, softened bevels, recessed neutral grout and seeded face tones. Quartic superellipse coverage and a separate smooth quadratic dome retain opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.6 m tile. No tile geometry, displacement or transparency.

`coffeerings` adds 3×3 seeded irregular elliptical coffee cup tide marks on pale paper, with locally faded brown rings and lighter interior staining. Pigment and roughness vary over near-flat paper grain without carved cavities or stain-driven AO. The 0.36 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no paper/cup geometry, liquid transparency, fluid, absorption or drying simulation.

`cashews` adds 4×4 cream kidney-shaped kernels with thick C-shaped bodies, connected rounded asymmetric tips and mouths open to the backing, in alternating ±45° orientations. Smooth crescent/tip unions and separate convex domes retain opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.16 m tile. No kernel geometry, transparent holes, subsurface scattering or food physics.

`hinges` adds 4×4 steel hinge faces with paired rounded mounting leaves, six recessed countersunk bores and five convex barrel knuckles. A continuous lower pin remains visible across four joint grooves; seeded brushed grain stays inside the hardware. The 0.4 m tile retains opaque metallic maps/metallic 1, optional height and ownership. No hinge geometry, transparent holes, articulation or rigid-body physics.

`coins` adds 4×4 gold coin faces with raised circular rims around lower fields and connected embossed five-point stars. Smooth overlapping triangular spikes and pentagonal hubs preserve open valleys without internal seams; seeded face tones alternate with 36° star orientations. The 0.12 m tile retains opaque metallic maps/metallic 1, optional height and ownership. No coin geometry, lettering, transparency, currency system or rigid-body physics.

`righttriangletiles` adds 32 blue-grey/cream right-isosceles glazed triangles in 4×4 split squares with alternating diagonal directions. Independent seeded pigments fade into recessed neutral grout; perpendicular diagonal distance keeps straight and diagonal grout/bevel widths equal. The 1 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership. No tile geometry, displacement or transparency.

`cherryblossom` adds 4×4 stylized pink five-petal flowers with shallow split-tip notches, a lower heart and twelve raised pale pollen dots. Independent smooth petal/dome unions preserve connected bodies without overlap spikes; seeded tones alternate with 36° orientations. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership. No flower geometry, transparent holes, growth, wind or subsurface-scattering simulation.

`punchcard` adds 2×4 thin cream paper cards with one clipped upper corner and 20×8 rectangular punch positions. Each column has two distinct seeded punches, leaving intact paper webs; slots return all surface fields to opaque backing rather than transparent cutouts. The 0.44 m tile retains dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership, with about 0.3 mm paper relief. No card geometry, real data encoding/reading or paper physics.

`braidedcord` adds four vertical cotton-colored flat plaits with four periods each. Three differently toned strands follow C2 quartic paths with doubled-phase depth, alternating over/under rather than forming the helices in `rope` or the two-strand knitted cable in `cableknit`. Smooth depth ownership preserves convex crossings and periodic column gaps. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no strand geometry, transparent holes, braiding process or cloth physics.

`crossbeddedsandstone` adds four horizontal sandstone beds containing gently curved inclined laminae with alternating dip directions. Seeded dip sets fade before the shared contact bands rather than continuing through bed boundaries, unlike nearly parallel `sandstone` strata and `rammedearth` layers. The 1.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership, with subtle lamina relief and continuous sandy grain. No rock geometry, displacement, stratigraphic generation, sedimentation or erosion simulation.

`grommetcanvas` adds 4×4 raised convex brass grommet rings on rough brown woven canvas, with dark opaque bore backing and smooth inner/outer bevels. The authored metallic map is 1 only on brass, 0 on cloth/backing and fractional on bevels; default material factor 1 preserves this split rather than making the whole surface metallic. The 0.16 m tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership. No geometry, transparent cutouts or cloth physics.

`fishscaletiles` adds interlocking teal glazed fan mosaics with convex semicircular tops, two concave lower arcs and neutral curved grout. Four columns and eight staggered rows retain seeded glaze tones and coplanar faces rather than overlapping roof shingles or painting waves on square tiles. The 0.4 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height, tint-neutral grout and ownership; no geometry, transparency or tiling simulation.

`hexkeys` adds 4×4 black oxide L-shaped hex keys with seeded quarter-turn poses, unequal arms, tangent round elbows and flat cut ends. Flat crowns and two side facets distinguish the hexagonal rods from round wire clips. The 0.4 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership; metallic map and default factor are 1. No tool geometry, mechanical assembly or physics.

`driedfigslice` adds 4×4 alternating tapered dried fig slices with tan wrinkled rind, dark brown flesh and many tiny pale elliptical seeds confined to the interior. Seed positions, axes and tones are deterministic and jittered rather than arranged in pomegranate chambers or a kiwi ring. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no fruit geometry, transparency, food generation or biological simulation.

`creamcrackers` adds 4×4 separate golden baked crackers with four scalloped borders, nine dark recessed docking pits, browned rims and irregular toasted blisters. Unlike continuous wafer pockets, each cracker retains an intact flat baked face between the nine pits. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no food geometry, transparent holes, baking or food physics.

`doorkeys` adds 4×4 flat brass door keys with seeded quarter-turn poses, bored circular bows, continuous necks, asymmetric blades, three rounded bittings, longitudinal grooves and chamfered tips. The 0.32 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership; metallic map and default factor are 1. These are stylized surface silhouettes, not key geometry, transparent holes, lock mechanisms or mechanical simulation.

`dodecagontiles` adds a canonical 3.12.12 glazed mosaic: 56 blue-teal dodecagons and 112 pale triangular inserts in seven columns and eight staggered rows, with seeded tones, neutral grout and coplanar faces. Its rectangular canonical period is normalized to square UVs with approximately 1% axis-scale difference, not a physical regular-polygon measurement guarantee. The 0.7 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no geometry, transparency or tiling simulation.

`ballchain` adds four silver ball pull chains with eight solid spherical beads each, narrow straight connectors and two gently meandering periods. Beads hide their lower connector joins instead of adding wire height through the crown; this differs from bored oval seed beads and ring links. The 0.064 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic map and default factor 1. No chain geometry, transparency or chain physics.

`succulentrosettes` adds 4×4 gray-green rosettes with three whorls of six fleshy spoon-shaped leaves each. Staggered inner leaves hide the narrower outer bases, rise toward the center and retain convex crowns rather than flat flower petals or parallel artichoke bracts. Seeded orientations and leaf tones vary the 0.64 m opaque dielectric tile. Existing maps, optional height and ownership remain unchanged, with metallic 0; no plant geometry, transparency, subsurface scattering or biological simulation.

`foldingfans` adds 4×4 alternating 140-degree paper fans with twelve radial accordion folds, thirteen exposed wooden ribs, side guards and round pivot caps. The annular paper hides the upper ribs while leaving open-looking gaps over opaque backing near the pivot. The 1 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no fan geometry, transparent holes, opening animation or folding physics.

`togglebuttons` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical wooden barrel toggles with tapered rounded ends, convex crowns, two recessed cord bores and gently curved longitudinal wood grain. It differs from the four-hole round rimmed `button` preset. The 0.32 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no button geometry, transparent holes, cords or garment physics.

`greenpeapods` adds 4×4 alternating diagonal closed green pea pods with curved pointed shells, two raised side seams, short stems and five covered pea bulges each. It differs from the longitudinally ribbed `cardamompods` and the round pitted `lotusseedpod`. The 0.48 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no pod geometry, transparent shells, opening animation or plant simulation.

`beltbuckles` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical brass belt buckles with tangent rounded frame corners, a lower center bar, a raised tapered tongue and a round pivot cap. The 0.32 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership; metallic factor and map are 1, including the lower dark backing that only suggests openings. No buckle geometry, transparent holes, belt material, fastening animation or mechanical simulation.

`hextruchettiles` adds 56 dark blue hexagonal glazed tiles with three ivory arcs each, seeded between two alternating-vertex orientations. All six edge midpoints connect across recessed neutral grout; the arcs remain flat pigment, not raised tubes. The 0.7 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0. Fitting the canonical 7×4√3 lattice to square UVs gives about 1% axis stretch; no tile geometry, metrology-grade regularity or path/puzzle gameplay.

`scoredmango` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical mango cheeks with intact oval skin rims, recessed crossing score cuts and convex golden flesh blocks clipped to the inner ellipse. Cut floors remain continuous above the skin; this is surface relief rather than actually everted or separated fruit pieces. The 0.48 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no fruit geometry, transparency or cutting physics.

`waxseals` adds 4×4 red wax seals with seeded irregular melted edges, recessed round impressions, raised circular rims and embossed five-lobed emblems. Angular edge/emblem masks have fully covered center guards, keeping the wax dome free of angular singularities. The 0.16 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no seal geometry, text/custom-emblem input, melting animation or wax-fluid simulation.

`cottonbolls` adds 4×4 open cotton bolls alternating by 45 degrees, each with four convex ivory fiber tufts, a lower shared center and pointed brown bracts exposed between the tufts. Normalized overlap heights preserve four separate crowns rather than summing them into one ball; seeded fine/nap fields suggest opaque fibers. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no fiber geometry, transparent fuzz, subsurface scattering or plant growth.

`carabiners` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical aluminum-colored asymmetric D-shaped frames, with straight spines, slanted gates, two recessed gate joints and raised hinge pins. Tangent exterior polygon offsets keep corners rounded; the apparent opening is lower dark opaque backing. The 0.32 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership; metallic factor/map are 1 including backing. No carabiner geometry, transparent holes, opening mechanism, collision or load-rating certification.

`pencilends` adds 4×4 hexagonal pencil cross sections alternating by 30 degrees, with thin dark painted rims, pale nearly flat cut wood and slightly lower solid round graphite cores. Paint, wood and graphite have separate roughness values; the core is not an empty hole. The 0.04 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no pencil geometry, sharpening/writing features or electrical simulation.

`corrugatedplasticedge` adds blue twin-wall plastic cut edges: eight half-cell-staggered board rows, each with eight rectangular flutes enclosed by two flat skins and straight webs. Their cut faces meet at one flush height, unlike the wavy paper medium of cardboardedge or closedcellfoam dimples. The 0.064 m opaque dielectric tile keeps metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; cavities are lower dark backing, not transparent holes. No board geometry, structural mechanics or plastic performance certification.

`haircombs` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical amber-colored plastic combs, each with a flat spine and fourteen tapered teeth ending in rounded shoulders. The roots remain flush with the spine; open-ended gaps reveal lower opaque backing, not transparent holes. The 0.48 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no comb geometry, transparent plastic, hair interaction or deformation simulation.

`blackberries` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical dark-purple glossy aggregate fruits. Nineteen convex drupelets in staggered 3/4/5/4/3 rows form each oval silhouette, with lower but filled pair/triple joints; this differs from grapeskin bloom patches and lychee polygons. The 0.16 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no fruit geometry, transparent flesh, plant growth or food physics.

`musselshells` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical blue-black teardrop shells with narrow hinges, wider rounded ends, solid convex crowns and fine curved growth channels. The chirped channels have variable spacing, unlike scallop radial ribs, irregular oyster laminae or cowrie toothed openings. The 0.32 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no shell geometry, bivalve opening, transparent shells or biological simulation.

`safetypins` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical steel-colored closed safety pins with round spring coils, two convex wire legs and raised solid guards covering the needle tips. Shallow clasp seams remain above the wire, and the loop/body gaps are lower opaque backing rather than transparent holes. The 0.16 m opaque metallic tile retains factor/map 1 including backing, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no pin geometry, opening mechanisms, spring physics or puncturing features.

`poppyseedpods` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical dry closed capsules with convex longitudinally fluted bodies and lower thin stalks. Raised, flattened eight-lobed stigma crowns carry radial ridges above the inter-ray plate and deep inter-lobe notches, unlike lotus seed chambers or pointed cardamom pods. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no capsule geometry, transparent skins, splitting, seed dispersal or plant simulation.

`leatherbookspines` adds eight parallel brown convex leather book spines with four curved raised binding bands, rounded blind-stamped frames and shallow panel floors. Fine pores remain visible between the compressed decoration; deep book seams separate the seeded spine tones. The 0.32 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership, with no text or metal foil; no book geometry, typography, page turning or binding physics.

`matchsticks` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical pale wooden square-ended shafts with longitudinal grain and wider, higher dark-red rounded coating heads. Rough head coatings differ from the nearly flat wood in pigment, height and roughness, replacing rather than thickening the overlapping shaft. The 0.2 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no match geometry, ignition, combustion, emission or chemistry simulation.

`porcelaininsulators` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical cream-glazed insulator side profiles with five rounded skirts, lower connected necks, domed caps and short stems. Outer skirt gaps expose lower backing while the central core remains solid; this differs from continuous fluted ceramic grooves or metal radiator tubes. The 0.8 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no insulator geometry, metal fittings, electrical-field/leakage simulation or insulation certification.

`dominoes` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical ivory rounded faces with two independently seeded standard 0–6 black pip arrangements and shallow center grooves. Pips remain filled recesses above opaque backing rather than alpha cutouts. The 0.24 m dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no domino geometry, game rules or physics.

`woodenspoons` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical wooden spoons with filled concave oval bowls, higher rims, connected necks, widening handles and longitudinal fibers. Bowl coverage replaces the lower handle rather than stacking relief, and the cavity remains above opaque backing; this differs from the beaded brushed handle surface of cutlery. The 0.4 m dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no spoon geometry, volume/fluid simulation or food-safety certification.

`chessrooks` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical ivory rook side profiles with three crown teeth, two outward-open notches, continuous narrowing shafts, collars and broad rounded bases. Bounded coverage and normalized component relief prevent overlap from stacking thickness; this differs from castellated hex nut rings. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no chess-piece geometry, board/rules/AI or physics.

`threadspools` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical dark-red wound barrels with lower continuous wooden cores, broader higher wooden end flanges, fine winding relief and rougher thread surfaces. Compact coverage replaces lower layers rather than stacking their thickness; periodic squared parabolic windings keep zero slope at joins. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no spool/yarn geometry, unwinding/tension simulation or cloth physics.

`bellpepperslices` adds 4×4 alternating green bell pepper crosscuts with a three-lobed fleshy ring, lower pale core/pith membranes, three open chambers and six convex attached seeds. Compact outer/inner circle unions form the lobed ring; pith and seeds stay within outer support, replacing lower layers rather than stacking relief. Chambers retain lower opaque backing, unlike cutout alpha. The 0.32 m dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no pepper geometry, transparent cavities or food/biological/fluid simulation.

`sharkteeth` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical ivory pointed triangular enamel crowns with fine serrations along both edges, lower rough double-lobed roots, continuous necks and outward-open root notches. `grinderburrs` adds 4×4 seeded steel burr rings with six curved higher smoother lands, lower rougher filled grooves, a central bore and three mounting holes. Squared-radius annulus/hole masks expose low opaque metal backing; integer flute count closes the angular branch and the bore masks its singular center. The 0.4 m opaque metal tile retains metallic 1 including backing, existing maps, optional height, matching material factor and ownership; no grinding geometry, cutting/coffee-grinding simulation or physics.

`cowhide` adds irregular rounded black-and-white patches over continuous short hair, distinct from zebra branching stripes, leopard rosettes and giraffe polygonal patches. The 0.8 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no hair geometry, grooming, cloth/biological or animal simulation.

`spaltedwood` adds pale longitudinal wood grain with sparse dark curved fungal zone lines. Unlike woodworm galleries, the lines are not carved cracks or missing wood. The 0.6 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no wood geometry, decay/fungal-growth/biological simulation or structural-safety assessment.

`canlids` adds circular stamped metal lids with a reinforcing bead, recessed score, raised twin-hole pull tab and higher rivet, distinct from crimped crowncaps. The 0.28 m opaque metallic tile retains metallic 1, existing maps, optional height and ownership; no can geometry, opening/tearing/deformation or mechanical simulation.

`crinoidossicles` adds pale circular stem fossils in continuous limestone matrix, with five-lobed sediment-filled central lumens and twenty fine radial articular ribs. `pythagoreantiles` adds a periodic Pythagorean tiling of two square sizes with a 2:1 side ratio, darker large squares, lighter small squares and shared recessed grout. Unlike windmillpavers, there are no rectangular pinwheel pavers. `dinnerforks` adds metal dinner forks with four tapered rounded tines, three opaque-backed slots and a broad head merging into a narrow neck and widened handle end. `goldbrocade` reuses damask's compact floral/leaf motif for raised exposed gold yarn over dark dielectric woven cloth. Exact product-complement unions keep connected bodies mortar-zero, red handles M0 and silver blades/necks/pivot M1; holes and exterior are low opaque M0 backing. Raised red handles are rougher than honed silver blades and lower than the pivot. A 0.25 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds static surface appearance, not geometry, moving hinges, cutting mechanics or safety certification.

`serpentinesprings` adds nine seeded single-trace silver serpentine spring wires: six straight runs, five alternating true semicircular U turns and two rounded free ends, not six detached bars or helical coils. Cached poses and 128² grain feed an exact piecewise squared-distance field. Straight rows, three right turns, two left turns and terminal owners change only in empty support; line/arc joins match value and first derivative even off the centerline. A single square root is used only for the eligible circular annulus, after culling its center and exterior; replacing it with an approximate implicit-ring distance would break that join. No per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or neighbor/run scan is used. Compact core/support profiles and a smooth rounded crown change wire height and roughness while retaining full-core M1 and exact mortar-zero; gaps remain low opaque M0 backing. A 0.2 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds a static flat serpentine-wire appearance, not geometry, helical spring construction, elasticity/force simulation or safety certification.

`impellerfaces` adds nine seeded silver impellers with six genuinely swept pointed blades, six open sectors, a higher annular hub and a true center bore, unlike a closed spur-gear rim. Cached poses and 128² grain feed squared-radius profiles and a sixfold angular phase warped by squared radius. Blade width tapers linearly in squared radius; products of smooth signed angular halfplanes meet at a true point without an abs-phase cusp or shrinking-width division. Phase-owner ties lie outside support, the full hub skips hidden blade angles/finishes and the origin never queries an angle. Each eligible blade annulus uses one atan2, with no per-point allocation, hash, sqrt, sin/cos or blade/neighbor scan. Product-complement hub/blade coverage retains M1 and exact mortar-zero on connected cores; bore and sectors are low opaque M0 backing. Rising radial blade height and a lower darker rougher hone remain below the hub. A 0.3 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds a static surface, not geometry, rotation, fluid/aerodynamic mechanics or safety certification.

`chipresistors` adds nine seeded rectangular black chip-resistor faces with two lower silver wrap terminals and a continuous white 103 marking, distinct from cylindrical banded axial resistors. Cached poses and 128² grain feed rounded body/terminal profiles, capsule strokes and a rounded-annular zero glyph. The zero counter reveals high opaque black body, not a hole through the component or low backing. Digit 3 uses an exact union of continuous vertical/horizontal strokes; nearest glyph/row ties and mirrored terminal ownership stay outside their supports. Ink widths account for rotated 512-pixel footprints. Full bodies skip hidden metal; each point performs no allocation, hash, sqrt, trigonometry or glyph/neighbor scan. Black body and bright white ink remain M0, silver terminals M1, with product-complement exact mortar-zero through the overlapping body/terminal cores. Ink adds a thin higher rougher layer; the body is above the terminals. A 0.12 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. The 103 marking is cosmetic, not a tested resistance, circuit simulation, precise component specification or electrical/safety certification.

`memorycardbacks` adds nine seeded generic keyed black memory-card backs with eight separate recessed gold contacts, seven high black insulating gaps, a truly clipped upper-right corner and an open right-edge key notch. Cached poses and 128² grain feed rounded body/pad profiles, a smooth diagonal halfplane and a rounded subtraction notch. Nearest-pad ties lie in contact gaps; every pad stays inside full shell support, clear of both key cuts. Empty shell skips pad/finish work; each point performs no allocation, hash, sqrt, trigonometry or pad/neighbor scan. Contacts are M1 and slightly lower/smoother than the high M0 black shell; pad gaps remain opaque full-height insulation, whereas corner/notch cuts expose low opaque M0 backing. Body coverage alone controls mortar, retaining exact zero through contact/gap transitions. A 0.15 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds static generic surface appearance without brands or logos, not storage access, circuitry, exact card dimensions, SD-standard compliance or security/electrical/safety certification.

`gnocchi` adds nine seeded cream floured oval pillows with rounded crowns and seven genuinely curved recessed fork impressions, not a palette-only ravioli or orecchiette variant. Cached poses, 128² grain and 48² flour fields feed a squared ellipse and dome plus seven compact capsules warped by x minus bend*y². Nearest-groove ties stay in intact dough outside compact supports; all impressions fit inside the full face. At the ends, the curved path shifts beyond the groove radius, so old straight-path controls remain high undented dough. Each point performs no allocation, hash, sqrt, trigonometry or groove/neighbor scan; outside support skips both noise finishes. Fork grooves lower/darken/roughen the dough and reduce AO without punching holes; the full face remains M0 and exact mortar-zero over low opaque backing. Flour affects pigment and roughness, not a particle simulation. A 0.12 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds static appearance, not geometry, cooking/growth simulation or food/safety certification.

`rectangularsocketfaces` adds nine seeded four-contact rectangular socket fronts with a closed silver bezel, genuinely low open cavity, high opaque black tongue and four higher silver contacts, not a memory-card palette swap. Cached poses and 128² grain feed squared rounded rectangles and capsules; outer-minus-mouth forms the continuous conducting ring. One nearest-contact owner is evaluated only inside the tongue; all pin supports fit in its full core, and the tongue fits inside the full mouth. Product-complement coverage keeps the full ring, tongue and contacts at exact mortar-zero; only bezel/pins are M1, while cavity/backing are low M0. Empty outer support skips mouth/contact/finish work, rim outside the mouth skips tongue/pins, and empty cavity skips grain. `saffronthreads` adds nine seeded groups of three curved red threads sharing one intact root, widened rounded tips and two genuinely low open fork gaps, not recolored straight rosemary needles. Cached poses and 128² grain feed three explicitly unrolled squared capsules warped by x minus sign*bend*(baseY-y)². Their common radius widens C1 near the tips; the quadratic warp stays unclamped across axial cap projections, avoiding off-axis derivative jumps. Both coverage and rounded crowns use product-complement unions, not max/nearest-branch cusps, keeping joined cores at exact mortar-zero and M0. Empty bounding support skips all capsule work and empty body skips grain. Each point performs three fixed capsule evaluations without allocation, hash, sqrt, trigonometry or neighbor scans. The core ratio is 0.71, retaining the existing differential guard with softer shoulders. A 0.06 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. `fishhooks` adds nine seeded continuous silver barbed J-hook fronts: a closed true-eye ring, straight shank, lower semicircular bend, tapered upper point and inward tapered barb, not a carabiner/safety-pin palette swap. Cached poses and 128² grain feed a squared shank capsule, circular inner/outer arc bands and eye disc, plus signed linear halfplane products for both tips. Product-complement unions connect the full cores without double height; one final bore subtraction cuts every component including the shank, preserving the low true eye opening. Tip planes meet smoothly without abs-width cusps or zero-width division. The central J cavity stays low all the way upward, and the inward barb remains distinct from an empty outward mirror. Full metal cores are M1 and exact mortar-zero; the bore/open cup/backing are low M0. Empty bounds skip all profiles, a full eye bore skips the other profiles, and empty body skips grain. Each point performs no allocation, hash, sqrt, trigonometry or segment/neighbor scan. A 0.15 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds static appearance over opaque backing, not hook geometry, fishing physics, strength or safety certification.

`palmiers` adds nine seeded golden heart-shaped pastries with two genuinely mirrored recessed caramel spirals, a central fold, notched double crown and tapered lower point, not concentric rings or a recolored cinnamon bun. `barbedwire` adds four seamless rows of two rounded silver strands with genuinely alternating over/under height, raised collars and four connected tapered barbs at each of sixteen knots. It reuses the existing C2 quartic-wave/tangent-corrected-width pattern, but quarter-phase depth forms a two-wire twist rather than doubled-depth figure-eight plaiting. Front/back alpha composition switches depth order only where the wire supports are disjoint, preventing both creases and doubled crossing height. Three cached row values hold phase/jitter/tone; 128² grain finishes occupied metal. Nearest-knot ties lie outside compact knot support while wires keep their own periodic phase. Full raised collars return before evaluating reflected barb owners, hiding their quadrant cusps; two signed tip halfplanes avoid abs-width tip creases and zero-width division. Derived bounds skip empty rows/profiles and empty body skips grain. One initialization sqrt bounds tangent widths; points perform no sqrt, trigonometry, allocation, hash or segment/neighbor scan. Full metal cores are M1 and exact mortar-zero; interwire/interbarb gaps remain low opaque M0 backing. A 0.4 m tile and 0.004 m relief preserve four maps, optional height, Worker/cache/tint and ownership. This adds static appearance, not fence geometry, mechanics or load/strength/electrical/safety certification.

`larimar` adds merging blue mineral pools, milky pale cloud veins and fine curved pectolite-like fibers, distinct from turquoise's dark matrix web or agate bands. `mulberrypaper` adds warm cream cloudy pulp with 192 randomly oriented long curved bast fibers and occasional tan inclusions, not woven strips or folded paper.
